3.Courtney didn't win the $18.25 pot.

6.The competitor who won with a full house was either Courtney or the player who won the seventh hand.

12.Of the person who won with a full house and the person who won the fifth hand, one was Courtney and the other won the $9.00 pot.

Determine the year each box was created, its country of origin, the wood type, and the craftsman who made it. Remember, as with all grid-based logic puzzles, no option in any category will ever be used more than once.  If you get stuck or run into problems, try the "Clear Errors" button to remove any mistakes that might be present on the grid, or the "Hint" button to see the next logical step in the puzzle.

Clues:
1.Chen's box was built sometime after the teak box.
2.Of Franz's puzzle box and the box from Germany, one was built in 1845 and the other is made of ebony.
3.The puzzle box from India was built 60 years after the box from Germany.
4.Of the puzzle box made in 1920 and Gustav's puzzle box, one is made of oak and the other is from England.
5.The puzzle box from Greece is made of mahogany.
6.The puzzle box from Turkey wasn't made by Franz.
7.Akira's box was built 30 years before the puzzle box from Turkey.
8.Of the puzzle box made in 1860 and the rosewood puzzle box, one is from China and the other was made by Chen.
9.The box from Greece was built 15 years after the box from England.
10.Emil's box was built 60 years before Gustav's puzzle box.
11.The seven boxes are the puzzle box made in 1890, Emil's puzzle box, Dimitri's puzzle box, the oak puzzle box, the bamboo puzzle box, the box from Japan and the mahogany box.

+---------+-----------+---------+----------+
|    1920 |           |         |          |
+---------+-----------+---------+----------+
|    1935 |           |         |          |
+---------+-----------+---------+----------+

Origins: China, England, Germany, Greece, India, Japan, Turkey
Woods: bamboo, ebony, koa, mahogany, oak, rosewood, teak
Makers: Akira, Bruno, Chen, Dimitri, Emil, Franz, Gustav,
